- **civitai-perf-review**: Reviews a feature segment in the main Civitai Next.js app (src/) for production performance — N+1 queries, unindexed scans, hot feed path regressions, cache stampedes, event-loop blocking, and client bundle weight. Use before calling a segment done, alongside civitai-reuse-review, civitai-correctness-review, civitai-test-review and civitai-intent-review. Only ever removes; never asks for code or scope. Use before calling a segment done, alongside civitai-reuse-review, civitai-correctness-review, civitai-perf-review, civitai-test-review and civitai-intent-review. Use before calling a segment done, alongside civitai-correctness-review, civitai-perf-review, civitai-test-review and civitai-intent-review. Read-only. Use when extract-intelligence dispatches the claim-verify lane for a freshly extracted module. ([FarhanRiuzaki/Mega-SDD](https://github.com/FarhanRiuzaki/Mega-SDD/blob/HEAD/plugins/mega-sdd/agents/claim-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **claim-verifier**: Verifies the CLAIMS a change makes about itself, not the code. Extracts every load-bearing assertion from a PR body, CHANGELOG entry or commit message — "X is gated", "this fails on Y", "nothing else does Z" — and checks each by running or grepping. A claim that cannot be checked is itself a finding. Read-only; it never edits. ([fmanimashaun/claude-skills](https://github.com/fmanimashaun/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/rails-flow/agents/claim-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **claim-verifier**: Adversarially checks a claim (or a small batch of them, one verdict each) against the repo and runtime — a review finding, an issue's "already done" or "still blocked" state, a doc statement, or a proposed close-on-proof — and returns CONFIRMED, PLAUSIBLE, or REFUTED with evidence. Use when a procedure calls for an independent verdict before acting. Read-only. ([frapplive/Frapp](https://github.com/frapplive/Frapp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/claim-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **claim-verifier**: Adversarially verify a claim against retrieved sources. Returns confirmed, refuted, or uncertain with decisive source excerpts; missing evidence remains uncertain. ([g-bastianelli/nuthouse](https://github.com/g-bastianelli/nuthouse/blob/HEAD/lore-hound/agents/claim-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **claim-verifier**: Read-only fact checker for a batch of claims that are about to go out in a message to other people (a team update, a client question list, a status report). For each claim it opens the primary sources (the client doc, the live or staging page, the code or data record, the PR) and tries to DISPROVE it, then returns confirmed | wrong | weak with quoted evidence, plus whether the item is something only the reader can answer or something we could fix ourselves. Used by the action-brief and pr-story skills before anything is drafted. Never edits, never posts. ([junielton/claude-base-dtk](https://github.com/junielton/claude-base-dtk/blob/HEAD/agents/claim-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **claim-verifier**: Use when a research claims ledger must be checked against its sources before a report is written; re-opens each cited source and locator and copies what it says, without seeing the claimed values. Dispatch one per candidate after the researchers finish. ([lthphuw/cv-kit](https://github.com/lthphuw/cv-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/claim-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **claim-verifier**: Same-family per-claim verifier for the primary-source-verification (psv) skill. Given ONE atomic claim AND the fetched source text that adjudicates it, returns a verdict (verified / refuted / narrowed / unverifiable) grounded in a fetched-source quote. Fresh, independent context; never edits or fixes. Spawn it per-claim as the verdict step (PSV-I3) of a psv run. The fetched source TEXT is the oracle — not model recall. ([maskshell/solidforge-dsh](https://github.com/maskshell/solidforge-dsh/blob/HEAD/preset/agents/claim-verifier.agent.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **claim-verifier**: Use to verify the factual claims in a doc (a CLAUDE.md, a lesson, a README) against the current codebase, and flag any drift. ([NAVNAV221/ai-native-codebase](https://github.com/NAVNAV221/ai-native-codebase/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/claim-verifier.md) · ★1)

- **claim-verifier**: Delegate to independently FALSIFY a claim, diagnosis, or "it works / it's fixed / it's a non-issue" conclusion BEFORE it is presented as fact.
